## Items for the paper, requested by the lead (2026-11-22)

Status: measured in this session, unchecked. Policies: MJWarp-Rough `3qymp37c`, RFT-Rough `6t0h2ud8`, MPM
`eayrj79r` (the earlier MPM checkpoint, not the retrained MPM-Rough). Forward command 1.0 m/s, ten trials per
cell. Velocity error and step time are means over successful trials.

### 1. Flat-ground and gentle-slope rows for the uphill sweep

MPM sand (beach sand preset), from `logs/t3/eval_2/report/summary.csv`:

| Slope | Goals MJWarp / RFT / MPM | Velocity error [m/s] | Yaw-rate error [rad/s] | Step time [s] |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 deg | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.04 / 0.10 / 0.04 | 0.37 / 0.26 / 0.36 | 0.24 / 0.27 / 0.33 |
| +5 deg | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.05 / 0.09 / 0.04 | 0.48 / 0.32 / 0.32 | 0.22 / 0.26 / 0.32 |
| +10 deg | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.07 / 0.10 / 0.05 | 0.57 / 0.38 / 0.25 | 0.21 / 0.25 / 0.29 |

On flat sand every policy reaches the goal and the velocity errors are 0.04 to 0.10 m/s. They are not equal: RFT
tracks worst on flat sand (0.10 m/s), and the step times already differ (0.24, 0.27, 0.33 s).

### 2. Step time and tracking error along the material axis

Hard end, flat rigid ground (MuJoCo Warp contact, no particles; OSMO `t3-rigid-flat-1`, script
`scripts/benchmarks/t3/eval_rigid.py`, 2026-11-22), next to flat sand:

| Ground | Goals MJWarp / RFT / MPM | Velocity error [m/s] | Step time [s] |
|---|---|---|---|
| Rigid ground | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.04 / 0.05 / 0.03 | 0.39 / 0.35 / 0.33 |
| Beach sand, flat | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.04 / 0.10 / 0.04 | 0.24 / 0.27 / 0.33 |

Sand grid, 7 densities x 6 frictions, 5 trials per cell and policy; every policy reached the goal in all 210
trials. Means over the other axis:

| Friction | Velocity error MJWarp / RFT / MPM [m/s] | Step time MJWarp / RFT / MPM [s] |
|---|---|---|
| 0.9 | 0.06 / 0.11 / 0.05 | 0.28 / 0.28 / 0.32 |
| 0.7 | 0.08 / 0.10 / 0.05 | 0.26 / 0.25 / 0.31 |
| 0.5 | 0.13 / 0.11 / 0.05 | 0.26 / 0.25 / 0.31 |
| 0.3 | 0.18 / 0.13 / 0.08 | 0.32 / 0.25 / 0.32 |
| 0.2 | 0.24 / 0.14 / 0.11 | 0.32 / 0.26 / 0.34 |
| 0.1 | 0.27 / 0.16 / 0.15 | 0.32 / 0.27 / 0.35 |

| Density [kg/m3] | Velocity error MJWarp / RFT / MPM [m/s] | Step time MJWarp / RFT / MPM [s] |
|---|---|---|
| 3000 | 0.15 / 0.12 / 0.11 | 0.29 / 0.26 / 0.33 |
| 2000 | 0.15 / 0.11 / 0.08 | 0.28 / 0.26 / 0.33 |
| 1000 | 0.15 / 0.12 / 0.07 | 0.28 / 0.25 / 0.32 |
| 500 | 0.17 / 0.13 / 0.07 | 0.31 / 0.25 / 0.31 |
| 250 | 0.21 / 0.17 / 0.08 | 0.33 / 0.27 / 0.32 |

Named soft materials, flat:

| Material | Goals MJWarp / RFT / MPM | Velocity error [m/s] | Step time [s] |
|---|---|---|---|
| Loose poppy seed | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.10 / 0.11 / 0.05 | 0.26 / 0.24 / 0.29 |
| Snow | 10 / 10 / 10 | 0.10 / 0.13 / 0.06 | 0.32 / 0.24 / 0.29 |
| Clay | 9 / 10 / 10 | 0.09 / 0.10 / 0.05 | 0.24 / 0.24 / 0.30 |

What the numbers support, and what they do not:

- **Hard ground: the policies do not differ in success or tracking.** All reach the goal, velocity error 0.03 to
  0.05 m/s.
- **MJWarp's tracking error rises as the sand gets slipperier:** 0.06 m/s at friction 0.9 to 0.27 m/s at friction
  0.1, a factor of 4.5. MPM rises from 0.05 to 0.15 m/s over the same range and RFT from 0.11 to 0.16 m/s. Along
  density MJWarp rises only from 0.15 to 0.21 m/s.
- **Step time from rigid ground to sand:** the baselines' steps get shorter (MJWarp 0.39 to 0.24 s, RFT 0.35 to
  0.27 s), the MPM policy's stay at 0.33 s. On rigid ground the simulated step times match the hardware values
  Junnosuke reported for MPM (0.33 s) and MJWarp (0.39 s).
- **Step time within sand is not monotonic for MJWarp.** It is shortest at friction 0.5 to 0.7 (0.26 s) and
  longest on the slipperiest and lightest sand (0.32 to 0.33 s). The paper should not say that the baselines'
  steps keep getting shorter as the sand gets softer; the supported statement is that the MPM policy's step time
  stays within 0.31 to 0.35 s over the whole grid while MJWarp's varies between 0.26 and 0.33 s and RFT's between
  0.25 and 0.28 s.
- **The MPM policy is not best everywhere.** At density 3000 kg/m3 its error (0.11 m/s) is close to RFT's (0.12).

### 3. Networks, observations and rewards, from the logged training configs

Read from the W&B configs of `3qymp37c`, `6t0h2ud8`, `eayrj79r` and the new run `aga5ovkp`, and from the
checkpoint weights.

Identical for all of them:

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Actor | MLP, 937 inputs, hidden layers 512, 256, 128, ELU, 29 outputs (joint position targets); Gaussian with one scalar standard deviation, initial value 1 |
| Critic | MLP, 1107 inputs, hidden layers 512, 256, 128, ELU, 1 output |
| Observation normalization | off |
| Actor observations | ten-step history of base angular velocity, projected gravity, joint positions, joint velocities and the previous action; plus the velocity command |
| Critic observations | ten-step history of base angular velocity, base orientation (quaternion), joint positions, joint velocities and the previous action; the velocity command; and privileged terms: base linear velocity, foot air time, foot contact, foot contact force, foot height, terrain material parameters |
| PPO | learning rate 1e-3 with adaptive schedule (target KL 0.01), 5 epochs, 4 mini-batches, 24 steps per environment, gamma 0.99, lambda 0.95, clip 0.2, entropy coefficient 0.005, 20,000 iterations |
| Reward terms and weights | the same 24 terms with the same weights: `track_lin_vel_xy` 4, `track_heading` 4, `track_ang_vel_z` 1, `foot_clearance` 5, `feet_air_time` 2, `base_height` -10, `flat_orientation_l2` -10, `stance_foot_angle_penalty` -8, `termination_penalty` -200, `no_fly` -2, `foot_distance` -2, `dof_pos_limits` -2, `joint_deviation` -1, `feet_roll` -1, `feet_roll_diff` -1, `feet_slide` -0.25, `ang_vel_xy_l2` -0.25, `lin_vel_z_l2` -0.25, `action_rate_l2_lower_body` -0.05, `action_rate_l2_upper_body` -0.05, `contact_impulse` -0.005, `energy` -0.001, `dof_vel_l2` -2e-4, `dof_acc_l2` -2.5e-7 |
| Robot-side randomization | added base mass, foot friction, actuator gains, pushes, reset pose and joints |

Differences between the policies:

| Item | MJWarp-Rough `3qymp37c` | RFT-Rough `6t0h2ud8` | MPM `eayrj79r` and the retraining |
|---|---|---|---|
| Environments | 1,024 per process; number of processes not verified | 1,024 per process x 4 GPUs | 64 per process x 40 GPUs = 2,560 |
| Symmetry | data augmentation and mirror loss 0.1 | the same | data augmentation only, no mirror loss |
| Contact signal for the gait rewards | rigid contact sensor | rigid contact sensor plus the RFT force | force fed back from the MPM coupling (plus MJWarp ground contact in the mixed task) |
| Foot clearance reference | ground under each foot, one height scanner per foot | the same | ground under the pelvis, one scanner, plus the 0.25 m sand depth on sand |
| Ground randomization | none | RFT friction, stiffness and density | sand friction, density, Young's modulus and yield pressure |

Two points for the paper text:

- **Observation noise is not applied in training, for any policy.** Noise ranges are configured (angular velocity
  0.2 rad/s, joint position 0.01 rad, joint velocity 1.5 rad/s), but `enable_corruption` is false in every logged
  config. Cause: the ten-step history observation group overrides the setup step that turns corruption on. The
  comparison is unaffected because it is the same for all policies, but the paper should not state that the
  policies are trained with observation noise. The projected-gravity term has a roll and pitch bias range of
  +-0.052 rad that is part of the term itself.
- **The pelvis-height reward target differs between rigid ground and sand** in the MPM tasks: 0.75 m above rigid
  ground, 0.50 m above the sand surface, because the height scanner sees the support under the sand and the term
  has no sand-depth offset. Left unchanged for the retraining by Junnosuke's decision of 2026-11-21.
<!-- T3-paper-items:end -->

## Methodology facts for the paper: locomotion (2026-11-22)

Requested by the lead for the paper's methodology. The values describe the MPM-Rough retraining
(`g1-mpm-mixed-cache-3`, W&B `gk4b6ci0`, and `g1-mpm-mixed-no-cache-3`, W&B `aga5ovkp`; task
`IsaacContrib-Velocity-G1-29dof-MPM-Mixed`) unless a row says otherwise. "Saved config" means the `env_cfg` and
`train_cfg` logged to W&B by the run. Config files are under
`source/isaaclab_tasks/isaaclab_tasks/contrib/velocity_mpm/config/g1_29dof/` in the unified checkout. Every row
is "read from config" unless it says "not sure".

### 1. Sand material randomization at reset

Source: saved config, `events.mpm_material` and `events.mpm_material_log`; file `env_cfg/event_cfg.py` lines 92
and 109. Sampled once per sand environment at every reset of that environment.

| Parameter | Range | Distribution |
|---|---|---|
| Friction coefficient | 0.3 to 0.9 | uniform |
| Grain (particle) density | 1,000 to 3,000 kg/m3 | uniform |
| Young's modulus | 1e7 to 1e9 Pa | log-uniform |
| Yield pressure | 1e8 to 1e9 Pa | log-uniform |
| Poisson ratio | 0.3 | fixed |

So four parameters are randomized, not two: the paper's "grain density and friction" leaves out Young's modulus
and yield pressure. The other material parameters stay at the preset's values. Rigid-ground environments in the
mixed task hold no sand and get no material randomization.

Baselines: MJWarp-Rough `3qymp37c` has no ground randomization. RFT-Rough `6t0h2ud8` randomizes the RFT ground at
reset (saved config): surface friction 0.1 to 1.0, stiffness coefficient 0.1 to 0.9, bulk density 1,000 to
3,000 kg/m3 with packing ratio 0.5 to 1.0.

### 2. Perturbations and robot-side randomization

Source: saved config `events` and `curriculum`; identical in `gk4b6ci0`, `aga5ovkp`, `3qymp37c` and `6t0h2ud8`.

| Item | Value | File |
|---|---|---|
| Pushes on the floating base | base velocity set to a sample of x and y within +-1.0 m/s, every 10 to 15 s | `env_cfg/event_cfg.py` line 128 |
| IMU roll and pitch bias | bias on the projected-gravity observation, roll and pitch each within +-3 degrees (+-0.052 rad); the range ramps linearly from 0 over the first 120,000 environment steps (5,000 iterations) | `env_cfg/observation_cfg.py` lines 44 to 48; `env_cfg/curriculum_cfg.py` lines 34 and 47 |
| Joint PD gains | stiffness and damping each scaled by a uniform factor in 0.9 to 1.1, all joints, at reset | `env_cfg/event_cfg.py` line 80 |
| Foot friction | static 0.6 to 1.0, dynamic 0.2 to 0.6, on the ankle-roll links, at start-up (64 buckets) | `env_cfg/event_cfg.py` line 28 |
| Added base mass | 0 to 5 kg added to the torso link, at start-up | `env_cfg/event_cfg.py` line 40 |
| Reset state | root position within +-0.5 m in x and y, any yaw; root velocity within +-0.5 m/s and +-0.5 rad/s per axis; joint positions scaled by 0.5 to 1.5 | `env_cfg/event_cfg.py` lines 55 and 71 |
| Velocity commands | forward -1.0 to 1.0 m/s, widened to 1.7 m/s after 240,000 steps and 2.5 m/s after 360,000 steps; sideways +-0.5 m/s; yaw rate +-0.5 rad/s, widened to +-0.7 and then +-1.0; 20 % of environments standing; resampled every 10 s | `env_cfg/curriculum_cfg.py` line 22 |

### 3. Training details for G1

| Item | MPM-Rough retraining | MJWarp-Rough `3qymp37c` | RFT-Rough `6t0h2ud8` |
|---|---|---|---|
| Environments per GPU | 64 | 1,024 | 1,024 |
| GPUs | 40 x NVIDIA L40S, one training process each | NVIDIA L40, 4 GPUs in the run metadata; number of training processes not sure | 4 x NVIDIA L40, one process each |
| Environments in total | 2,560, of which 1,280 on sand and 1,280 on rigid ground | 4,096 if four processes; not sure | 4,096 |
| Iterations | 20,000 | 20,000 | 20,000 |
| Steps per environment per iteration | 24 | 24 | 24 |
| Transitions per iteration | 61,440 | 98,304 if four processes; not sure | 98,304 |
| Control and physics step | 50 Hz control, 200 Hz physics (dt 0.005 s, decimation 4), 20 s episodes | the same | the same |

Sources: saved configs (`scene.num_envs`, `num_steps_per_env`, `max_iterations`, `sim.dt`, `decimation`,
`episode_length_s`); GPU type from the W&B run metadata; node count from the OSMO workflows (40 single-GPU
trainers). The sand share is `scene.terrain.sand_env_fraction` = 0.5, file `streaming_mpm_mixed_env_cfg.py`.

Status on 2026-11-22: the no-cache run is training. The cache run stopped at iteration 4,667 with a GPU
out-of-memory error while recording a video; it is being fixed and resumed from its iteration-4,500 checkpoint.
A resumed run restarts its curricula, so the no-cache run is the clean one.

### 4. Observation noise

Observation noise is configured but not applied in training, for any of the policies: `enable_corruption` is
false for the policy observation group in every saved config. The paper should not state that the policies are
trained with observation noise. The roll and pitch bias of item 2 is part of the observation term and is applied.

### 5. Network

| Item | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Actor | MLP, 937 inputs, hidden layers 512, 256, 128, ELU, 29 outputs | `agents/rsl_rl_ppo_cfg.py` lines 16 to 34; saved `train_cfg`; layer shapes read from the checkpoints of `3qymp37c`, `6t0h2ud8` and `eayrj79r` |
| Critic | MLP, 1,107 inputs, hidden layers 512, 256, 128, ELU, 1 output | the same |
| Same for all three walking policies | yes | saved configs and checkpoints |
| Towing policies | hidden layers 512, 256, 128 with ELU in the tow agent configs (`contrib/push/config/g1_29dof_mpm_tow/agents/rsl_rl_ppo_cfg.py` and `g1_29dof_rigid_tow/agents/rsl_rl_ppo_cfg.py`) | read from the config files. Input sizes of the towing policies: not sure, not read from a checkpoint; ask the T2 session |

### 6. Go2

There is no Go2 walking task or training run on the unified code. `contrib/velocity_mpm/config/go2/` holds only a
note file (`task.md`), no task registration, and there is no Go2 project in W&B.
